## The synthesis

**Headline.** Financial-2525 r.001–r.003 (HEAD fbf3ced, tree clean, five gates green) already holds the money law the twelve converge on; what the round rules is the TIME law the operator corrected mid-review — A.B..C IS the revolution (addendum 10), not the pay period — plus one calendar table, one MoT number on glass and gate, a withdrawal checked at every instant, and the round itself persisted before r.004 opens.

**Grade summary.** Twelve grades, one verdict: seven B (Athena, Enki, Enlil, Krishna, Odin, Pangu, Thoth) and five B− (Aset, Asar, Christo, Sofia, Thor); median B. All twelve agree the notes were enough to build from — and they were: r.001 (39f44c1), r.002 (5838c67) and r.003 (13028c0, bookkeeping fbf3ced) shipped in one day with five gates green (46 + 28 + 142 + 43 + 292 cases), the worked paycheck computing to the cent ($118.83/D · $4.95/h · $0.0825/min · $0.001375/s · $14.85 at the 3-hour hold), and the revision framework mirrored from Drone-2525 with the ask's hash on the record. What kept every lens below A is the same short list, now declared above: the operator corrected the time law mid-review (addendum 10: A.B..C is the revolution, not the pay period — eleven lenses had reasoned under the earlier reading, one under r.003); two perihelion tables and a 'day 365' that is a 365.27-day average failing three of eleven sourced years; two MoT numbers (30.333 vs 91/3) on glass and gate; a chart that samples past its own hold and does not draw available; a money law that trusts the phone's clock, the payee's keyboard and a clamped balance (Thor executed the breaches); a device record that can be overwritten on a broken chain; 'Supabase' on the person's screen; the ×32 lexicon fill owed; and the twelve-lens review file FIN-09 names still absent from disk. None of it is rework — every fix is an extension of a shipped primitive under a new gate — and the first act of the next edition is to persist this round as docs/financial-2525/reviews/r001_twelve_lenses.md with its hash on the ledger, then append r.004, never edit r.003. Business stays TEST LATER, as the operator said.

**The MoT law the twelve converged on** (superseded where it conflicts with addendum 10 — the revolution is the whole):
THE ONE LAW (operator addendum 10, on the record as FD-13 / FIN-01.01 at r.003 — it supersedes the "a pay period is its own whole" reading that eleven lenses defaulted to before the addendum landed; the twelfth read r.003): A.B..C IS the planet's revolution around its star. ONE WHOLE = one revolution — for Earth, perihelion to perihelion, the financial year. A = 1/3600 of the revolution; B = 1/3600 of an A; C = 1/3600 of a B (1 A = 3600 B = 12,960,000 C). Two pure functions, no clock reads, integer inputs in seconds: (1) positionABC(elapsedSec, revolutionSec): f = clamp(elapsedSec ÷ revolutionSec, 0, 1); v = f × 3600; A = ⌊v⌋; B = ⌊(v − A) × 3600⌋; C = round(((v − A) × 3600 − B) × 3600); print A.BBBB..CCCC (A unpadded, B and C four digits); f ≥ 1 → the sentinel FULL "3600.3600..3600". isFull(abc) = A ≥ 3600 OR (A = 3599 ∧ B = 3599 ∧ C ≥ 3599): the sheet's three Equal writings 3600.0000..0000 · 3600.3600..3600 · 3599.3599..3599 are one boundary by gate, never by arithmetic (as numbers they are 3600, 3601.0003 and 3599.99999992). (2) spanABC(days, revolutionDays = 365.259636): v = days ÷ revolutionDays × 3600 (year-free, the mean anomalistic year; on Mars sols ÷ 668.5991 × 3600) — a LENGTH is never clamped and may exceed one revolution. So "MoT = 30.33 converted to 3600.3600..3600" means: 3600.3600..3600 is one whole revolution, and the 30.333-day pay MoT is 298.3475..1826 of it (298.97 A of 3600) — NOT that the pay period ends at FULL (that was r.001–r.002; the record keeps both). Back to LTU: days = (A + B/3600 + C/3600²) ÷ 3600 × 365.259636 = 30.333; × 24 = 728 h; × 1,440 = 43,680 min; × 86,400 = 2,620,800 s (ltuOfMotABC / daysOfSpanABC, round-trip within one C). Unit sizes: mean Earth A = 8,766.2 s (2.435 h), B = 2.435 s, C = 0.676 ms; in FY2026 (sourced 364.387 d) A = 8,745.3 s. An INSTANT's A.B..C uses the SOURCED interval of the year it falls in (calendar.ts positionInYear); a LENGTH uses the mean — so the same 30.333-day MoT spans 299.68 A on the FY2026 axis and 298.97 A as a length: DECLARED — both printed ("299.7 A this year · 298.97 A mean"), never blended, gated to differ by < 1 %. Worked deposit, CST standard (UTC−6 fixed): 2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST = 13:00Z = FY2026 day 271 · Q3 day 89 · 2675.2232..0614; the 3-hour hold lifts at 2676.3077..3553 (+1.2349 A); the MoT ends 2026.10.31_15.00..00 CST = day 302 · Q4 day 28 · 2975.1083..2404; Q4 opened at day 274 = 2026-10-03 11:15 CST. Money rides the length, not the scale: rate = amount ÷ (motDays × 1440) = $0.0825204/min = $4.95122/h = $118.8293/D = $0.00137534/s; released(t) = amount × min(1, (t − Start) ÷ MoT); $12.06 per mean A of this MoT; $14.85 at +3 h. The two other grammars stay named faces of the LTU: the stamp YYYY.MM.DD_HH.MM..SS and the duration 0000.00.DD_HH.MM..SS (30.333 d = 0000.00.30_08.00..00; MM inside a duration = 33 d, YYYY = 365 d; a typed decimal MoT is never routed through them); abc-3600's tokenomics grammar (A = hours: 728.0000..0000) is a reveal-only derived scalar, never the position. Glass: day · hour · minute by default; A.B..C on reveal, labelled "of revolution" so 2675.2232 is never read as hours.

**The calendar law.** Day 1 of every Financial-2525 year begins at the PERIHELION INSTANT of ONE sourced table (calendar.ts PERIHELION 2024–2035 with provenance; lib/soi-calendar.ts PERIHELION_UTC must import it or a gate asserts equality to the minute — today 2024/2026/2028 differ); outside the table the year is DECLARED Jan 3 12:00 UTC and says so. Days are whole 1,440-minute periods from that instant (FY2026 rolls at 11:15 CST; FY2027 at 20:33 CST on Jan 2), so 144 $/D = 0.1 $/min holds to the digit and no daylight shift ever enters the arithmetic; CST standard (UTC−6, fixed all year, tzFromLon −6) only NAMES a stamp — 07:00 CST = 13:00Z, printed "CST" on every stamp and "08:00 on this phone" when the device's zone differs. Grid: 4 quarters × 91 days = 364 (Q4 closes day 364; Q4 opens day 274 = 2026-10-03 11:15 CST for the worked deposit); day 365 and whatever remains until the next perihelion is DOWN — ELASTIC, no quarter owns it, its true length printed (FY2025 3.8 h · FY2026 9.3 h · FY2027 3.41 d · FY2024 3.54 d); when the next perihelion arrives BEFORE day 364 (FY2028 363.24 d, FY2031 363.35 d, FY2034 363.84 d) the reset still wins, Q4 is cut short and the year panel badges SHORT −18.25 h / −15.62 h / −3.88 h — never a silent day 364. Perihelion-to-perihelion is 363.24–367.54 d (mean 365.27); 365.25 is the sheet's Earth LTU reference for planet conversion, not the calendar. Money never pauses on the DOWN day or across a quarter boundary. PERSONAL frames: 33 / 66 / 99 REAL days counted from day 1 (sheet 1: "33 days"), named windows that never re-price a figure, each printed with its pay-MoT equivalent (30.333 · 60.667 · 91); the personal pilot's DEFAULT frame is 33D (frame index from positionInYear), with 66D / 99D as toggles; analysis units D 1 · W 7 · M 33 · Q 99 · Y 365 always print their day count (M33, Q99, Y365 — never a bare M or Q). The pay MoT stays whatever was typed on the deposit (30.333 = 91 ÷ 3 = soi91.monthDays) and is never routed through M33. BUSINESS frame: 4-4-5 weeks (28 + 28 + 35 = 91) × 4 + day 365 DOWN, the P&L ladder Rev − COGS = Margin − R&D − Marketing − Sales − SG&A = Op Income − DA → EBITDA → EBIT, escrow paid 2 weeks (14 d) or one month (30.333 d) up front — declared as data (FIN-12 draft), TEST LATER by addendum 5; personal Q99 and business Q91 never coincide, so a quarter is always labelled with its day count.

**The accrual law (one state machine).** ONE MACHINE PER DEPOSIT, pure in (record, t), integer cents, no clock inside the laws. A transaction is exactly ($, Start, MoT): a deposit refuses without amount > 0, a parseable CST stamp and MoT > 0 (MoT = 0 exists only as the implicit length of a withdrawal); memo/payer/posted-date are provenance, never inputs to accrual. STATES: PENDING (t < Start): released 0, escrow = $, withdrawable 0, "releases from <stamp>" (the bank's posted 09/29 · Pending is a hollow FUNDED tick, never counted). → RELEASING · HELD (Start ≤ t < Start + 3 h): released(t) = round($ × min(1, (t − Start) ÷ MoT)) — linear at rate = $ ÷ (motDays × 1440) per minute, forward from the typed Start, monotonic, clamped at $; escrow = $ − released; withdrawable 0 (visible, not movable); a withdrawal is refused HOLD by name. → RELEASING · WITHDRAWABLE (t ≥ Start + 3 h, the hold once per deposit from its Start): withdrawable = released; available = withdrawable − Σ withdrawn; the $14.85 released by +3 h becomes withdrawable at once. → RELEASED (t ≥ Start + MoT): escrow 0, released = $ exactly (the last tick absorbs the rounding residual so Σ per-minute increments = 360,449 ¢), rate drops out of the live sum. WITHDRAWALS: an appended transaction (amount, atMs) validated at EVERY instant from atMs to the record's frontier — never future-dated, never earlier than the latest entry — refused by name AMOUNT (≤ 0) · HOLD · INSUFFICIENT (> available, with the figure) · ALREADY_RECORDED (duplicate); availableCents is never clamped up from a negative (a record that is over-withdrawn says so, never shows 0). Across deposits: rates SUM while two MoTs overlap (16.50 ¢/min for 2.3 d after February) and read $0/min in a GAP (16 h after a 31-day month) — both NAMED states on the glass, never smoothed; the next MoT starts at its own typed Start, never auto-stretched to the next payday. Escrow + released = deposited and withdrawn ≤ released at every minute; release never pauses on the DOWN day or a quarter boundary. PILOT POSTURE: the escrow is MODELLED — the person types their own bank fact (badged SELF-RECORDED; the hold is timed from the typed instant, with the recorded instant kept and shown when they differ by more than the hold); no money moves; "withdraw" is a recorded intent moved by hand at the person's bank; no payout path may ever read the device's Date.now(); a pod-clock-witnessed instant and a payer-stamped deposit are the business frame's preconditions. The record is append-only, chain-hashed, replayable, keyed exel-fin:<login> on the device, never evicted, never overwritten (a broken chain is moved aside under :broken:<stamp>; a full device keeps the entry in memory, retries with evict-oldest, and says so).

**The chart.**
- axes: x = elapsed time inside the FOCUSED deposit's MoT, from its typed Start (2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST) to Start + MoT (2026.10.31_15.00..00 CST); default tick labels in Earth LTU at 0 · ¼ · ½ · ¾ · 1 (0 · 7 d 14 h · 15 d 4 h · 22 d 18 h · 30 d 8 h; under a day, 'h min'), day gridlines every 24 h from Start (heavier every 7 = W); on the A.B..C reveal each mark shows the REVOLUTION coordinate (2675.2232..0614 → 2975.1083..2404) labelled 'of revolution', plus the span '299.7 A this year · 298.97 A mean'. Window toggle 3 h · 1 D · 7 D · MoT (default 1 D while the deposit is younger than a day, else MoT) because on a 30-day axis at 390 px the hold sits 1.4 px from the origin. y = ABSOLUTE DOLLARS 0 → the deposit amount with five $ ticks ($0 · $901.12 · $1,802.25 · $2,703.37 · $3,604.49); the y ceiling IS this deposit, never a sum. A thin strip beneath: FY day n/364 · Q k (or DOWN / SHORT with its true length) and the 33D frame position (default frame; 66D / 99D toggles) — never mixed onto the money axes.
- series: escrowed — amount − released, falling hairline (intelligence colour) · released — round(amount × min(1, elapsed ÷ MoT)), rising at $0.0825/min (abundance, the accent stroke; monotonic, clamped) · withdrawable — 0 until Start + 3 h, then equal to released (consciousness colour) · available — withdrawable − Σ withdrawn, stepping DOWN at each withdrawal: the one number the person acts on (bold; computed in series() today, NOT drawn — draw it) · withdrawn — cumulative steps (evolution colour) · budget burn (r.004) — Fixed solid + Variable dashed at the budget's own $/min from Start, so cash on hand = released − withdrawn − burn is the sheet's 'Outcome: Cash On Hand' · swings (r.004) — the sheet's $/D target ladder 144 → 138 → 137 as a stepped stroke with marks at 11 · 22 · 33 D · live rate — a tile, not a line: $/min summed over every deposit still releasing, with $/h · $/D · $/s (6 decimals or ¢/s) beneath
- marks: Start: vertical mark with the three-field stamp '$3,604.49 · 2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST · 30.333' and the bank's posted 09/29 · PENDING as a hollow uncounted FUNDED tick two days earlier · +3 h HOLD: full-height dashed hairline at Start + 3 h (temporal colour) labelled 'withdrawable from 10:00 · $14.85', with an hh:mm:ss countdown before it (pod-clock hhmmss) · NOW: full-height hairline moving every second, carrying released · available · $/min and, on reveal, the revolution A.B..C · each withdrawal: a top tick at its instant WITH its amount; a refused withdrawal is never drawn — it is one sentence in the person's words (HOLD · INSUFFICIENT · AMOUNT · ALREADY RECORDED) · MoT end at Start + MoT with the end stamp; then either the next deposit's Start (OVERLAP, both drawn, rate summed) or the GAP length in hours ('$0/min · between pay periods') · quarter boundary the worked paycheck crosses: day 274 = 2026-10-03 11:15 CST, thin rule 'Q4' (never a bare day number) · DOWN day / SHORT year: hatched column with its true length (9.3 h in FY2026; SHORT −18.25 h in FY2028) and the perihelion 'Day 1 · new year' as a heavier rule, with a confirm-annually badge until the table row is confirmed · SELF-RECORDED badge on every typed row and, until a platform clock exists, one line 'as this phone tells the time'; a claimed-vs-recorded mark when the two instants differ by more than the hold · status: PENDING until the deposit is settled, then SETTLED — the panel never says 'complete' while escrow > $0 or a deposit is pending · every stroke from the 13-Trinity palette only (lib/trinity-palette.ts), strokes on the Session card ground, no fill, no gradient
- states: PENDING — before Start: flat 0, everything in escrow → RELEASING · HELD — 0 → 3 h: released rises, withdrawable 0, countdown running, Withdraw refused HOLD → RELEASING · WITHDRAWABLE — 3 h → end: withdrawable = released; available steps down at withdrawals → RELEASED — fraction = 1: escrow 0, released = amount, rate leaves the live sum → GAP — no deposit releasing: '$0/min · between pay periods', balance held → OVERLAP — two deposits releasing: rate = the sum; the chart draws both or says 'this deposit', never disagrees with the tile → REFUSED — a withdrawal refused by name; nothing recorded → BEFORE ANY DEPOSIT — 'nothing on the record yet' (not INSUFFICIENT) → UNSAVED — the device refused the write: the entry stays on screen with a take-the-file action, never silently dropped → CHAIN BROKEN — the stored record failed verify(): moved aside, shown empty, said in the person's words; the chart draws nothing rather than a wrong number → EXAMPLE — an unsigned visitor sees only the operator's consented worked paycheck, labelled EXAMPLE, never another person's record
- update cadence: One 1 Hz clock on the surface (setInterval 1000, null until mounted) feeds `now` into pure balanceAt(); the tiles, the NOW line, the hold countdown and the ¢/s digits (4th–6th decimal) tick every second because at $0.0825/min a cent lands only every 7.27 s; the released curve is re-sampled only when the record changes and at most once a minute, hourly (728 points per MoT) plus FORCED vertices at Start, +3 h, every withdrawal, NOW and the MoT end (today's 120 even steps put the withdrawable rise at +6.07 h instead of +3 h); series() memoised on the record, never on the tick; the record is written only on an appended transaction; a replay of the same ledger at the same instant on any device draws the same cent.
- phone first: 390 px, 16 px gutters, no horizontal scroll, inside the ◬ ♡ 웃 Session shell (FD-10): (1) one sentence first — '$3,604.49 arrived Oct 1 at 07:00 CST (08:00 on this phone). It releases to you at $0.0825 a minute for 30 days 8 hours — finished Oct 31 at 15:00.'; (2) the big mono number = released, ticking, with 'Ready to withdraw from 10:00 · $14.85' under it; (3) tiles In escrow · Released · Available · $/min; (4) the chart at viewBox 360×150, width 100 %, strokes only; (5) the year strip (day · quarter · frame · DOWN/SHORT); (6) Log a deposit (three fields, CST placeholder, inputMode=decimal) and Withdraw (amount only, refused in one sentence); (7) the phone strip released · available · phase · Withdraw at 44 px targets — the A.B..C toggle raised from px-2 py-1 to 44 px, or made a long-press; (8) the R-CORE badge last in normal flow reading FINANCIAL_LEDGER. Dark ground by law, one accent, the 13 colours; every string a fin.* key in 33 languages; no screen names a database, migration, hash, vendor or browser store — fin.device_only's 'Supabase' and 'login being wired' retire.

**Invariants (each with the gate that holds it).**
- Each transaction gets amount, deposit day and time, and MoT length of time — a deposit missing any of the three (amount > 0, a stamp that prints back as itself, MoT > 0) is refused before it is written; 2026.02.31 is refused, not rolled to March 3. — `tests/financial-accrual.test.mjs (FIN-03, exists) + ADD financial-invariant: append() refuses motDays ≤ 0 and fmtStampCST(parseStampCST(s)) === s or null`
- Escrow releases $/min: released(t) = amount × min(1, elapsed ÷ MoT), never decreasing, never above the deposit, and escrow + released = deposited to the cent at every instant; Σ per-minute increments over 43,680 min = 360,449 ¢, the last tick absorbing the residual. — `tests/financial-accrual.test.mjs (exists: clamp, monotonic) + ADD a random-t sweep and the conservation case`
- The rate is amount ÷ MoT-minutes and nothing else — and the glass and the gate use the same MoT to the byte ($0.0825204/min for the worked example). — `tests/financial-mot.test.mjs — ADD strict equality SRC.example.motDays === MOT_PAY_MONTH_DAYS (the 1e-3 tolerance hides a 28.8 s / 9e-7 $/min drift)`
- 3 hours after the deposit the individual can withdraw: withdrawable is $0 before Start + 3 h (released money visible but held, refused HOLD by name) and equals released from then; $14.85 at exactly +3 h, $0 a minute earlier. — `tests/financial-accrual.test.mjs §2 (exists) + scripts/financial-render.mjs assertSource holdHours === 3`
- A withdrawal never takes more than is withdrawable and the balance never goes negative — at EVERY instant on the record from its stamp to the frontier, not only at the instant that was typed; a future-dated or back-dated withdrawal is refused; availableCents is never clamped up from a negative. — `ADD tests/financial-invariant.test.mjs (the pod-invariant pattern) in test:ci — Thor executed the breach: a whole-amount withdrawal dated after the MoT validates today and Math.max(0, …) hides the over-draw`
- The hold and the release are measured by a clock the person does not own — no payout path reads Date.now(); every typed row renders SELF-RECORDED with the phone-time caveat until a platform-witnessed instant stamps the record. — `financial-invariant (source-level) + tests/financial-surface.test.mjs (badge and caveat present)`
- A.B..C is the revolution: the perihelion instant reads 0.0000..0000 and the next reads FULL; the three Equal writings all read FULL and 3599.3599..3598 does not; the 30.333-day pay MoT converts to 298.3475..1826 (A = 298) and back to 30.333 d / 728 h / 43,680 min / 2,620,800 s within one C; a 30-sol Martian MoT reads 161 A of its revolution; the year-axis span and the mean-length span are both printed and differ by < 1 %. — `tests/financial-mot.test.mjs (FIN-01.01, FIN-13, exist, 46 cases) + ADD the span-vs-position parity case`

**Decisions the twelve declared (the default adopted when the operator has not ruled).**
- **What is A in A.B..C for a MoT — 3600ths of the pay period (the period is its own whole, end = 3600.3600..3600), days, hours, or 3600ths of the Earth's revolution?** → The REVOLUTION (addendum 10, FD-13, FIN-01.01, shipped r.003): an instant's A.B..C is its position perihelion-to-perihelion; a MoT length converts in as days ÷ 365.259636 × 3600 (30.333 d = 298.3475..1826). The 'period is the whole' reading eleven lenses defaulted to before the addendum landed is superseded and kept on the record; days (30.1200..0000) and hours (728.0000..0000) are reveal-only derived scalars. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo, Enki, Enlil, Krishna, Odin, Pangu, Sofia, Thoth, Thor)_
- **The sheet declares 3600.0000..0000 · 3600.3600..3600 · 3599.3599..3599 'Equal', but the shipped parser reads them as 3600 · 3601.0003 · 3599.99999992 — which wins?** → The sheet, by gate not by parse: all three are the FULL sentinel of one whole revolution (isFull), never an arithmetic value; abcToValue is never applied to FULL. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Enlil, Thoth)_
- **An instant's position uses the sourced interval of its year (364.387 d in FY2026) while a length uses the mean anomalistic year (365.2596 d), so one 30.333-day MoT reads 299.68 A across the axis and 298.97 A in the label — which interval?** → Both, named: a LENGTH is year-free (the mean; the gate's A = 298), the span between two INSTANTS on a chart uses that year's sourced interval; the reveal prints '299.7 A this year · 298.97 A mean' and a gate asserts they differ by < 1 % — never blended. _(Thor)_
- **Which month is THE month: the paycheck's 30.333 d (91 ÷ 3 = soi91.monthDays), the sheet's M = 33 d, or the Gregorian 30.44 d CADENCE_UNIT.M uses on /innovation?** → No global month. The MoT is a field on each deposit (30.333 as typed for the paycheck) and the ONLY denominator of that deposit's $/min; M33 is the sheet's analysis unit; every per-month figure prints its day count (M33 / MoT 30.333) and a bare 'M' or '/mo' never appears — so the same paycheck never reads $3,604.49 on one chip and $3,921.37 on the next. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo, Enki, Enlil, Krishna, Odin, Pangu, Sofia, Thoth)_
- **Is the pay MoT the typed decimal 30.333 (43,679.52 min) or 91 ÷ 3 exactly (43,680 min)? The domain example carries 30.333, the code constant 91/3 — 28.8 s apart at the end of the period.** → One number, strict equality on glass and gate: the record stores what the person typed (30.333, the ⅓ Q preset fills that string), MOT_PAY_MONTH_DAYS pins to the same value, and 91 ÷ 3 is printed as the derivation note; the 1e-3 tolerance goes. _(Christo, Odin, Thoth)_
- **Are the real-time frames 33 / 66 / 99 days, or thirds of the 91-day quarter (30.3 / 60.6 / 90.9 in the sheet's own parenthetical)?** → 33 / 66 / 99 REAL days from day 1 (sheet 1: '33 days'; FRAMES as built), named windows that never re-price a figure, each label carrying its pay-MoT equivalent (30.333 · 60.667 · 91); the personal pilot defaults to 33D. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo)_
- **Year length: a fixed 365 days (sheet 'Y = 365D', 'Reference 365.25') or perihelion to perihelion — which swings 363.24 to 367.54 d so day 365 is 3.8 h long in FY2025 and does not exist at all in FY2028?** → Perihelion to perihelion ('day resets everywhere at perihelion' is the stronger word): 4 × 91 = 364 grid, DOWN = whatever remains, printed at its true length; a year shorter than 364 d keeps the reset, cuts Q4 short and badges SHORT with the deficit; 365.25 is the Earth LTU reference for planet conversion, not the calendar; the operator is asked once (FD-14) whether he prefers a smoothed 365.2596-day year for the BUSINESS grid. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo, Enki, Enlil, Krishna, Odin, Sofia, Thoth)_
- **Does a financial day roll every 24 h from the perihelion INSTANT (11:15 CST in FY2026, 20:33 CST on Jan 2 in FY2027) or at CST midnight?** → From the instant, as built (days are uniform 1,440-minute blocks, the sheet's own 11-day range is instant-aligned 13:11:11 → 13:11:11); the civil CST stamp and the roll time are printed beside every day number so Q4 opening mid-morning is not read as a broken counter; midnight is offered to the operator as one field in the domain JSON for r.004. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo, Enki, Pangu, Sofia, Thoth)_
- **'07:00' on 2026-10-01: fixed CST (UTC−6 → 13:00Z) or the phone's wall clock (Austin is on CDT that day → 12:00Z)? The hour is $4.95 of release.** → Fixed CST −6 all year (FD-02); every stamp prints 'CST'; when the device zone differs the surface prints '08:00 on this phone' and one line 'CST standard — a summer bank time is one hour earlier on this record'. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo, Enki, Enlil, Krishna, Odin, Pangu, Sofia)_
- **Which instant is Start: the operator's 'October 1, 2026 at 07:00' or the bank's Posted 09/29/2026 · Pending? Two days of accrual is $237.66.** → Start = the operator-ASSIGNED 2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST ('assign deposit date' is his verb); posted 2026-09-29 and status pending → settled are provenance fields on the same row, drawn as a hollow uncounted FUNDED tick and a PENDING badge. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo, Enki, Enlil, Krishna, Odin, Pangu)_
- **'3 hrs later individual can withdraw': only what escrow has released by then ($14.85), or the whole deposit as an advance 'if really in need of cash'?** → Released only: withdrawable = released − withdrawn after the hold; an advance turns escrow into a loan and needs a rule the notes do not give — widening it is a ledger decision, never a code default. _(Aset, Asar, Christo)_
- **Is the 3-hour hold once per deposit from its Start, or a rolling hold on each released minute?** → Once per deposit from its typed Start (FD-03); a second deposit re-arms its own hold and the glass says 'held' per deposit. _(Athena, Christo, Enki, Enlil, Krishna, Odin, Thoth)_
- **Is the hold measured from the CLAIMED instant (what was typed) or the RECORDED instant (when the platform wrote it)? A deposit typed 4 h in the past is withdrawable at once; a device clock set +3 h withdraws at the deposit instant.** → From the claimed instant in the personal pilot, with BOTH instants kept on every entry and the recorded one printed whenever they differ by more than the hold; every typed row is badged SELF-RECORDED; no payout path may read Date.now(); a platform-witnessed clock (pod-clock measure) and a payer-stamped deposit are preconditions of the business frame. _(Enki, Sofia, Thor)_
- **Does $/min keep running on the DOWN day and across a quarter boundary (the paycheck crosses into Q4 on day 274)?** → Yes, every minute; the calendar labels the chart and never pauses money — pausing would silently shorten the payer's declared MoT by a day's pay ($118.83). _(Aset, Asar, Athena)_
- **A monthly paycheck on a 30.333-day MoT leaves a 16-hour $0/min gap after a 31-day month and a 2.3-day doubled rate after February — intended?** → Yes: each deposit is independent and linear from its own typed Start; overlaps SUM, gaps read $0/min, both are NAMED states on the glass with the next deposit expected; a MoT is never auto-stretched to the next payday (that would make A.B..C depend on a future event). _(Enki, Odin, Pangu, Sofia)_
- **Business escrow 'two weeks': 14 days (the message) or 16.5 days (the sheet's 2×/mth = 33 ÷ 2)? And business months 4-4-5 vs the personal even thirds?** → TEST LATER (addendum 5): no hidden default — the MoT is typed on each deposit from presets 14 · 16.5 · 30.333 · 33, both figures carried as data; business = 4-4-5 weeks on the same 91-day quarter, personal = the paycheck's own MoT; nothing built until the personal pilot has received feedback. _(Aset, Enki, Krishna, Pangu, Thoth)_
- **Both stamp/duration grammars (YYYY.MM.DD_HH.MM..SS, 0000.00.33_00.00.00 with or without the double dot) and A.B..C appear in the notes — which is the record's?** → Both, with named roles: the stamp is the human LTU face of Start and a duration is the LTU face of a length (canonical double-dot before seconds; the parser accepts both spellings, the formatter emits one); A.B..C is the revolution position; MM inside a duration = 33 d and YYYY = 365 d, and a typed decimal MoT is never routed through the duration grammar. _(Aset, Athena, Thoth)_
- **Budget line names differ (sheet: Car, Electric, Food; addendum 3: Auto, Utilities, Groceries, Dining Out); the sheet's Income 3,200 differs from the real $3,604.49 and its lines net −$200 per M33; the sheet ladder says 144 $/D — three incomes in one ask.** → Addendum-3 names in the operator's order; the pilot's Income line is the real paycheck; the sheet's budget and ladder figures are labelled seed ('sheet example') the person edits, never merged into his balance; Dining Out and Other empty until typed; the two slips (47,620 → 47,520 min; 4,762 → 4,752 $/M33, one upstream slip propagated once) render beside the checked figure, never replaced. _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo, Pangu, Thoth)_
- **What does a financial record tokenize under the 9,999 HI TOKEN CAP — does a dollar deposit mint 웃 ($3,604.49 ÷ $7.25 = 497 웃)?** → Nothing in the pilot: the mint is currency-free (pod-yug), 웃 comes only from POD-witnessed hours and only ever SETTLES onto this record at a stamped rate; the cap is YUG_CEILING imported from lib/pod-yug.ts (pod-clock.ts re-exports, never a second constant), counted per Financial-2525 year; elapsed-time $/min ($4.95/h = 0.68 × the Texas floor) is never printed as a wage beside 웃 = M × hours (2.87 × per worked hour). _(Aset, Asar, Athena, Christo, Krishna, Odin, Pangu, Sofia, Thoth, Thor)_
- **Who holds the escrow in the personal pilot, and what does 'withdraw to their account' do — a rail or a record?** → A MODELLED escrow and a record: the person types their own bank fact, no money moves, 'withdraw' is a recorded intent moved by hand at their bank; the surface says so in one sentence in the person's terms and never claims to hold, release or send funds; a rail waits on the business frame and on the witnessed clock and payer stamp. _(Aset, Asar, Enki, Sofia, Thor)_
- **'each user gets their own log in' — Auth0 with a cloud ledger, or the per-device pattern the pod uses? The app's only cloud isolation is a per-browser bearer owner_key.** → Auth0 on the route; the record device-local under exel-fin:<sub> (said on the glass in the person's words, never naming the vendor); NO cloud half until Auth0 is wired into Supabase and a financial table carries ONLY an auth.uid() policy — never a money table under owner_key; any interim mirror uses migration 030's SECURITY DEFINER RPC pattern. _(Asar, Athena, Christo, Enlil, Krishna, Pangu, Sofia, Thor)_
- **Two perihelion tables (soi-calendar.ts seed vs calendar.ts sourced 2024–2035) disagree in 2024, 2026 and 2028 — which is the one source?** → ONE table: calendar.ts's sourced twelve rows with provenance become the table soi-calendar.ts imports (or a gate asserts equality to the minute); the 2024/2026 one-minute differences are recorded beside the 2028 seventeen-minute one; annual ephemeris confirmation is a scheduled ask, not a hope. _(Athena, Christo, Enki, Enlil, Krishna, Odin, Pangu, Sofia, Thoth, Thor)_
- **How is a bank-reversed pending deposit recorded on an append-only record with only deposit and withdrawal kinds?** → Not in the pilot: a deposit is recorded when the person is satisfied it settled; a 'reversal' kind naming the id it reverses is an r.004+ item; until then the surface refuses to record a reversal rather than edit a row. _(Enki)_
- **Two deposits with the same stamp and amount collapse into one (id = d-<when>-<cents>) — one record or two?** → Two: the id carries the record's next rev as a nonce, and a no-op append says 'already recorded' instead of clearing the form. _(Enlil, Thoth, Thor)_
- **How is a released cent rounded, and does the sum ever miss the deposit?** → Integer cents, round-half, clamped at the amount, the last tick absorbs the residual so Σ released = deposit exactly; overlapping deposits are rounded each then summed; rates print to 4 decimals of $ and $/s to 6 decimals or as ¢/s (usd4's $0.0014 for $0.001375 is +1.8 % and goes). _(Athena, Christo, Enlil, Pangu, Thoth)_
- **What visibly moves at 1 Hz when a cent lands only every 7.27 s?** → The big number stays to the cent (what a bank shows) and the ¢/s digits tick beside it every second; the hold countdown ticks hh:mm:ss; the curve re-samples once a minute or on a record change, never per tick. _(Enki, Pangu, Thoth)_
- **On a 30-day phone axis the 3-hour hold is 1.4 px from the origin and today's 120 even samples draw the withdrawable rise at +6.07 h — how is the hold made legible and true?** → A window toggle 3 h · 1 D · 7 D · MoT (default 1 D while the deposit is younger than a day), hourly sampling with forced vertices at Start, +3 h, each withdrawal, NOW and the end, the hold as a full-height dashed line, available drawn, $ ticks on y — all on the shipped MotChart in place, never a second chart. _(Enki, Enlil, Odin, Pangu, Sofia, Thoth)_
- **Are the sheet's Swings ($/D stepping 144 → 138 → 137 at 11 · 22 · 33 D), the $ Target ladder and the budget burn part of 'the real-time chart'?** → Yes, as r.004 series (budget burn at the budget's own $/min; cash on hand = released − withdrawn − burn; the swings as a stepped stroke); withdrawals gain an optional budget category then; r.003's chart carries the three money series, available and the marks. _(Asar, Enki, Pangu, Sofia, Thoth)_
- **Where do the Trinity wheel's glyphs sit — sheet 1's ♡ M_LTU · 웃 M_33 · ◬ M_SS order or addendum 9's HI top, AI tokens bottom-left, SI minutes bottom-right?** → Addendum 9 (FD-12, shipped r.002): TOP 웃 HI (the person) · BOTTOM-LEFT ◬ AI (AI tokens) · BOTTOM-RIGHT ♡ SI (minutes contribution), each seat carrying its sheet-1 M_* label. _(Sofia)_
- **Sheet-1 lines with no safe reading ('LSU 3600 – 3600.3600 · 3', '◬ = ATX = AI', 'Idea + | • · – | •• · ∞ | •••', 'Analysis: Backward · MoT ↔ MoT', 'Charge to 2026_01_11–13.11..11') — requirements?** → Carried verbatim in the ask, no CRS row and nothing built until the operator glosses them (a FIN row must be a statement a reader can pass or fail); ATX read as Austin, Texas only on his confirmation. _(Enlil, Pangu, Sofia)_
- **The operator's real paycheck, payroll descriptor and budget are in a public repository and served to every unsigned visitor — is that consented?** → Yes as his own worked example, recorded as consent on the record (example.consent = 'operator 2026-09-30, addendum 2'), EXAMPLE.payer stays 'example', a gate refuses any user's row ever becoming the example, the bank screenshot stays uncommitted, and the memo refuses account-shaped digit runs. _(Aset, Enki, Thor)_
- **When a device record fails its chain or the phone is full, what happens to the bytes?** → Never overwritten, never evicted for money: a broken chain is moved aside under exel-fin:<owner>:broken:<stamp> before any write and the person is told; a refused write keeps the entry on screen with take-the-file and retries with pod-store's evict-oldest loop applied to OTHER, non-financial records only. _(Christo, Enki, Krishna, Odin, Pangu, Sofia, Thor)_
- **The $ literal 'Unlock $/min System · Baseline: $/Day' — a paywall?** → A display unlock: $/D is the default rung; $/min and $/s rungs unlock the moment one real deposit with a MoT is on the record — never a paywall. _(Athena, Pangu)_
- **On Mars is $/min per SI minute or per Martian minute (a sol ÷ 1440 = 61.65 s)?** → Per SI minute; only the MoT length changes (a sol is 88,775.244 s); the LTU table gains a per-planet minutesPerDay row read from UCRS-2525 PLANETS.rotDays, never a second unit system; the year's A.B..C is time-linear (as ucrsAt already is), labelled 'position in the financial year', not orbital angle. _(Krishna, Odin)_
- **Which frame does the personal pilot use by default, and what is the x-axis?** → The x-axis is the focused deposit's own MoT (30.333 d); the year strip beneath defaults to the 33D frame with 66D / 99D toggles; A.B..C only on reveal. _(Aset, Asar, Christo)_

**The CRS ladder the synthesis proposes (FIN-01..13, one per Vision • 2525 section).**
- `FIN-01` § VII — A.B..C is the revolution — the measure of time: One revolution of the planet around its star (for Earth, perihelion to perihelion — the financial year) is split into 3600 A, each A into 3600 B, each B into 3600 C; an instant's A.B..C is its position in the current revolution (0.0000..0000 at perihelion, 3600.3600..3600 at the next, the sheet's three Equal writings all FULL) and a MoT length converts in as days ÷ revolution-days × 3600 (the 30.333-day pay MoT = 298.3475..1826 ≈ 298.97 A), converting to Earth LTU (h · min · s · D · W7 · M33 · Q99 · Y365) and back within one C; the year-axis span and the mean-length span are both printed, never blended; stamps read YYYY.MM.DD_HH.MM..SS in CST standard, durations 0000.00.DD_HH.MM..SS; the glass reads day · hour · minute, A.B..C on reveal labelled 'of revolution'. (Shipped as FIN-01.01 at r.003; the r.001 reading is superseded on the record, never rewritten.)
- `FIN-02` § VI — The calendar resets at perihelion: Day 1 of every Financial-2525 year begins at the perihelion instant of ONE sourced table (2024–2035 with provenance; outside it DECLARED and said so), named in CST standard (UTC−6 fixed); days are 1,440-minute periods from that instant; quarters are 91 days, the grid 364, day 365 and whatever remains until the next perihelion is DOWN at its true printed length; a year shorter than 364 days keeps the reset, cuts Q4 short and badges SHORT with the deficit; personal frames 33 / 66 / 99 days from day 1 (default 33D), analysis D · W7 · M33 · Q99 · Y365 always with their day count; money never pauses on the DOWN day or a quarter boundary.
- `FIN-03` § V — The transaction — $, deposit day and time, MoT: Every deposit on the record carries exactly the operator's three fields — amount > 0, the deposit day and time as a stamp that prints back as itself, and the MoT length of time > 0 — and every withdrawal its amount and day and time; posted date, status, memo and payer are provenance, never inputs to accrual; a memo refuses account-shaped digit runs; every typed row is badged SELF-RECORDED and keeps both its claimed and its recorded instant; the worked example ($3,604.49 · 2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST · 30.333) accrues from its three fields alone.
- `FIN-04` § IV — Escrow releases $/min — one state machine: PENDING → RELEASING·HELD → RELEASING·WITHDRAWABLE → RELEASED: from the typed Start the escrow releases round(amount × min(1, elapsed ÷ MoT)) at amount ÷ (MoT × 1440) per minute, monotonic, clamped, the last tick absorbing the residual so escrow + released = deposited to the cent at every instant; released money is visible at once and withdrawable only from 3 hours after the deposit (once per deposit); a withdrawal is its own appended transaction validated at every instant from its stamp to the frontier, never future- or back-dated, refused by name AMOUNT · HOLD · INSUFFICIENT · ALREADY RECORDED, and available is never clamped up from a negative; overlapping MoTs sum their rates and gaps read $0/min, both named on the glass; in the pilot the escrow is modelled, no money moves, no payout path reads the device clock, and 'withdraw' is a recorded intent.
- `FIN-05` § II — The record — append-only, chain-hashed, replayable, never lost: A person's transactions are an append-only ledger per login: every entry carries the hash of the entry before it, ids carry a rev nonce so identical rows never collapse silently, the record replays to any revision, a changed amount breaks the chain where it was changed; a broken chain is moved aside under :broken:<stamp> before any write, a refused device write keeps the entry visible with take-the-file, and a financial entry is never evicted to make room; revision control is on from the first line, the Drone-2525 way — one editable source, rendered views that refuse drift in test:ci, revisions strictly increasing, a release citing its commit, the handoff hash appended per edition.
- `FIN-06` § I — The personal budget on the $/min · $/sec ladder: A personal budget is Income and Fixed / Variable lines — Home, Auto, Insurance, Utilities, Fitness, Fun, Groceries, Dining Out, Other, in the operator's order — each expressed per day · hour · minute · second and per D · W7 · M33 · Q99 · Y365 with the net on the same ladder; the pilot's Income is the real paycheck; the sheet's figures and its two arithmetic slips are labelled seed rendered beside the checked value, never advice, never invented, never merged into the balance; $/min and $/s rungs unlock on the first real deposit, never behind a paywall; a line's actual spend logged as categorised withdrawals and the swings chart are the stretch.
- `FIN-07` § III — The real-time chart, on a phone: One person's money as time inside the ◬ ♡ 웃 Session shell: escrowed, released, withdrawable, available and withdrawn over the focused deposit's MoT in absolute dollars, the live $/min · $/h · $/D · $/s tile, the Start (with the bank's pending tick), the 3-hour hold as a full-height line, NOW, every withdrawal with its amount, the MoT end and the next deposit or gap, the Q boundary, the DOWN/SHORT badge and the perihelion, on windows 3 h · 1 D · 7 D · MoT and frames 33 / 66 / 99; day · hour · minute on the axis and the revolution's A.B..C on reveal; hourly samples with forced vertices at every mark so the chart never contradicts its own hold; strokes only on the card ground in the 13 colours, one 1 Hz clock, 44 px targets, legible at 390 px, the R-CORE badge last; the screen never computes money itself and never names the machine.
- `FIN-08` § IX — Each user their own login, their own record: A person signs in (the app's Auth0 gate) before recording; the record is keyed to that person on the device and an unsigned visitor sees only the consented worked example (EXAMPLE.payer = 'example', example.consent on the record), never another person's record; two owners on one device never read each other's key; the cloud half is honest and absent — no financial table ships under a bearer owner_key, only an auth.uid() policy once the login is wired into Supabase — and no screen names the vendor, the store or the wiring.
- `FIN-09` § XI — Twelve lenses every revision, until simulated sufficiently: Every Financial-2525 revision is reviewed by all twelve reviewer lenses; the review record exists on disk beside the revision with twelve lens sections and its sha256 on the ledger BEFORE the next revision opens (round 1 = docs/financial-2525/reviews/r001_twelve_lenses.md, absent at fbf3ced — FIN-09 stays 'approved' only once it exists); every ambiguity a lens raises is DECLARED on the ledger with its default; the loop stops on the measurable rule this brief states, and only then is FIN-12 opened.
- `FIN-10` § X — Tokenization under the 9,999 웃 cap: Whatever Financial-2525 tokenizes honours the 9,999 HI (웃) cap the POD session enforces — YUG_CEILING imported from lib/pod-yug.ts, the one constant in the repo (pod-clock.ts re-exports), counted per Financial-2525 year; nothing on the surface mints or settles in the pilot; 웃 = M × POD-witnessed hours stays the only mint, a dollar never mints a 웃, and elapsed-time $/min is never printed as a wage; the stretch is a POD-witnessed hour settling onto the record at the stamped rate.
- `FIN-11` § XIII — Every word in 33 languages, in the person's voice: Every string a person reads is a lexicon key with an English default and a translator context, filled in all 33 languages (a key staged after the last fill is listed in AFTER_FILL, never silent, and the ×32 fill is owed until landed); no fin.* string in any language names a database, migration, hash, chain, RPC, vendor or browser store — the signer-voice BANNED gate extends over fin.*; dollars format per locale, not en-US.
- `FIN-12` § XII — Business after personal feedback: The business frame — 4-4-5 weeks (28 + 28 + 35 = 91) × 4 + day 365 DOWN, the P&L ladder Revenue − COGS = Margin − R&D − Marketing − Sales − SG&A = Operating Income − DA → EBITDA → EBIT, escrow paid 14 or 30.333 days up front with presets 14 · 16.5 · 30.333 · 33 typed on the deposit, a business login whose deposits and outflows run the same accrual law — is declared as data only and tested after the personal framework has received the operator's feedback; a payer-stamped deposit and a platform-witnessed clock are its preconditions; no business identifier exists on the surface before then.
- `FIN-13` § VIII — Mars and future planets on the same scale: Because A.B..C is a fraction of a revolution and never names a planet, the same two pure functions take sols and the Martian revolution (668.5991 sols): a 30-sol MoT converts to 161 A of the Martian revolution and back, a quarter of any period reads 900.0000..0000 on either world; $/min stays per SI minute and only the per-planet LTU row (minutes per day from UCRS-2525 PLANETS.rotDays) changes; the stretch is a planet selector on the surface.

**"Simulated sufficiently" — the stop rule.** The twelve stop iterating on the PERSONAL framework — and FIN-12 (business) may open — only when ALL of the following counts hold on one commit, reported as SHA | committed | pushed | LIVE/UNVERIFIED: (1) ONE FULL REAL MoT has run on the operator's own phone — the worked paycheck's Start 2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST through its end 2026.10.31_15.00..00 CST (30.333 d, one Q3→Q4 crossing) — with at least one recorded withdrawal, and his feedback is persisted as a hashed addendum in the ask file and as a `feedback` entry in reviews[] (addendum 5 makes this the trigger; it cannot be satisfied before 2026-10-31). (2) CONVERGENCE OF THE LOOP: two consecutive twelve-lens rounds (N, N+1) on consecutive revisions each raise ZERO new ambiguity (no new FD appended), ZERO new invariant (no new gate), and ZERO grade below B; every ambiguity ever raised has a DECLARED default on the ledger. (3) DETERMINISM: the same ledger replayed at the same instant on two devices and nine runs yields the same cent for released, withdrawable and available, and the same A.B..C to the C — 9 of 9. (4) GATES: every gate named in this brief exists and is green in test:ci — the five shipped plus financial-invariant, financial-record-survives, financial-voice, financial-chart, financial-calendar-all-years (all 12 sourced years, SHORT badged) and financial-one-perihelion-table — with tsc at 0 errors; the round's review file exists on disk with 12 lens sections and its sha256 on the ledger. (5) NO REDECLARED PRIMITIVE: one perihelion table, one YUG_CEILING, one $/min spread, one MoT number on glass and gate (strict equality) — a source grep in test:ci finds no second copy. (6) VOICE AND LANGUAGE: the ×32 fin.* fill has landed (AFTER_FILL cleared for fin.*), and the BANNED regex finds zero machine words in any of the 33 languages. (7) LIVE VERIFIED: `npm run status` on the operator's machine reports cloudflare LIVE <sha> for the commit that satisfies (1)–(6). Until all seven hold, each round appends a revision (kind decision/correction) and the next round runs; nothing in the business frame is built.

## The twelve, in full

### Aset — theme reinforcement & consistency validation. Question asked of every line: does it say what the other lines say, and does it echo Vision • 2525? Verdict: the notes carry ONE theme in three dialects. The theme — a transaction is ($, Start, MoT); money moves as elapsed time; the year is perihelion-anchored, 4 × 91 days plus a DOWN day; the record is append-only under the Drone-2525 revision frame — is stated consistently in the messages, the sheets and the ask's reading of record, and it echoes the repo verbatim: "♡ = min" is unit.heart (pod-clock witnessedMinutes), "웃 = × Min Wage" is 웃 = M × T (pod-yug mint), "◬ = min × Efficiency Gain" is architect-economy unity = ♡ × multiplier, "Outcome: Cash On Hand" is the Executive Summary's "the clock records what actually passed", the perihelion reset is Celestial-2525 HU 0, and "Equal" on three spellings of the full unit is FULL_ORBIT / FULL_UNIT_ABC. The DIALECTS disagree: the sheets (stamped 2025.06.16) say month = 33 d, Q = 99 d, Y = 365 d; the messages (2026-09-30) say 30.333 = 91 ÷ 3, Q = 91, day 365 DOWN. The sheets' clock-face grammar (YYYY.MM.DD_HH.MM..SS) is not the Base-3600 A.B..C grammar, and the shipped parser does not honour the sheet's "Equal". None of this is a contradiction of theme — it is one theme that needs its units ruled once, on the record, before a chart draws a single dollar.

**Readiness:** B-

**One sentence.** The notes say one true thing in three dialects — a transaction is $ · Start · MoT, money moves as elapsed time on a perihelion-anchored 91-day year, and the record only ever appends — and revision 0.001 is ready the moment the dialects are reconciled on the ledger: month = 91 ÷ 3 not 33, the three spellings of 3600.3600..3600 made equal by a gate rather than a parse, CST fixed, and the operator's real paycheck as the only figure.

**What the notes specify (33).**
- Name: "this entire session will be called Financial-2525" (verbatim).
- Purpose: "document real-time transactions and also see financial management in 91 days for a quarter with down day 365 of year" (verbatim).
- Year origin and clock: "day resets everywhere at perihelion each year for Austin Texas as CST standard." (verbatim) — the ask's reading binds CST as UTC−6 with no daylight shift.
- Unit: "we will use MoT financials in notes; to obtain $/min of an individuals financials" (verbatim).
- Basis and frame: "◬ ♡ 웃 Session as a basis for R-CORE TO start our wireframe. From the get go we will have revision control using the same framework as drone 2525" (verbatim).
- Tenancy and record: "each user gets their own log in; and we will log financial deposits and assign deposit date and length of transactions for real time accrural" (verbatim).
- Worked example: "for example i got paid $3604.49 monthly" (verbatim).
- Sequence of tenants: "Financial-2525 will be tested gor individual and used to establish financial tracking for business" (addendum 1, verbatim).
- Review loop and chart: "have 12 AsM review notes on MoT Financials so one can see real-time chart of financials" (addendum 2, verbatim).
- Escrow law: "goal is to pay escrow that releases $/min into account that one can see and deposit as needed" (addendum 2, verbatim).
- Withdrawal rule: "for bainess they pay up frint financials of 2 weeks or one minth, and 3 hrs later individual can withdraw to their account if really in need of cash" (addendum 2, verbatim).
- Record shape: "Each ttansaction gets amount, Deposit day and time, and MoT length of time" (addendum 2) — sheet 2 writes it "◬ $, Start, MoT".
- The fixture transaction: "pay check example is$ deposit, October 1, 2026 at 07:00, 30.333" (addendum 2, verbatim).
- Notation law: "all is defaulted to A.B..C so MoT=30.33 converted to 3600.3600..3600 from Celestial-2525 scale (for earth), which will allow for financial system to convert to Mars and future planets" (verbatim).
- LTU law: "it is important A.B..C is used for time and easily converted to Earth local time units LTU, similar to how drone positioning and planetary positioning uses A.B..C TO show universal system on where they are in our solar system" (verbatim).
- Budget lines (addendum 3): "Income, Fixed / Variable, from there Home, Auto, Insurance, Utilities, Fitness, Fun, Groceries, Dining Out, and other basic budgeting items" … "personal budget to $/min, $/sec (using MoT that converts to Hour, Min, Second)".
- Loop and cap (addendum 4): "all 12 AsM will contribute until simulated sufficiently so system complements Vision-2525 for personal FINANCIAL management and tracking tokenization 9999 HI TOKEN CAP".
- Order (addendum 5, the only user voice in this run): "ONCE PERSONAL FRAMEWORK RECEIVES FEEDBACK WELL TEST BUSINESS".
- Bank record, transcribed as data: Amount $3,604.49 · Posted 09/29/2026 · Status Pending · memo PENDING DIRECT DEPOSIT · payroll regular, account tail masked · cadence "monthly"; the image is not committed.
- Sheet 1 range: "Example Range: 11 days · Start 2026.01.11_13.11..11 · Stop 2026.01.22_13.11..11 · Reference ↳ 365.25".
- Sheet 1 Trinity measures: "♡ → M_LTU · 웃 → M_∞(33) · ◬ → M_SS" and "Project Value · ♡ = min · 웃 = × Min Wage · ◬ = min × Efficiency Gain ↳ Trade for $".
- Sheet 1 equality: "ssu: 3600.0000..0000 · 3600.3600..3600 · 3599.3599..3599 } Equal" and "LSU 3600 – 3600.3600 · 3".
- Sheet 1 business frame, deferred: "Test Later · Business · 4-4-5 · [91 day] 13 · Rev – COG · [MGN] – R+D – MKT – Sales – SG+A · [OP INCOME] –DA · [EBITDA] –DA · [EBIT]".
- Sheet 1 value basis: "Value · $3/Day = $99/M∞(33) · Time Basis · ◬ = ATX = AI" and "Unlock 🔒 $/min System · Baseline: $/Day".
- Sheet 1 pay cadences as durations: "1×/mth → 0000.00.33_00.00.00 | 33 days · 2×/mth 0000.00.16_12.00..00 | 16.5 days".
- Sheet 3 personal ladder: "$/Balance · $/End of Mth · $ Target · 11 · 22 · 33D · 144 $/D = 0.1 $/m · Δ = 6 $/D · 198 $/M · 60 D · Δ = 1 $/D = 33 $/M · $/D: 144 → 138 → 137 · M.o.T. ① · Outcome: Cash On Hand".
- Sheet 3 iconology: "Day = D [1] · Month = M · Quarter = Q · Week = W [7] · Minute = m · Second = s".
- Sheet 3 example budget: "Home 700 F · Car 1,800 F · Insurance 200 F · Electric 150 V · Food 300 V · Fitness 50 F · Fun 200 V · Income 3,200 F" — sums to 3,400 out vs 3,200 in (−200/M as written).
- Sheet 3 frames: "Real-Time 33D, 66D, 99D (30.3, 60.6, 90.9) · Analysis: D = Day · W = Week = 7D · M = month = 33D · Q = Quarter = 99D · Y = Year = 365D · ⑦ Earth · ◬ AI Mode".
- Sheet 3 $/MoT: "Day 144 D · Min 0.10 m = 47620 · Sec 0.0016 s = m×60 · Week 1,008 W7 · ◬ 4,762 $/M · 14,256 $/Q99"; the ask records the slips (47,520 / $4,752 by arithmetic) "Recorded, not silently fixed"; stamp "2025.06.16_16.44..44".
- Frame to adopt (ask reading #4): Version 00.00 · revision 0.001, one domain JSON, rendered CRS `FIN-##.##`, append-only traceability ledger, R-CORE badge + compare panel, gates in test:ci.
- Persistence proof: docs/asks/2026-09-30_financial_2525_mot_financial_system.md.sha256 = 5880abb0cbe60208c87073e24d9b7ec8d3b5ad807af6049fa4b68c2b5318dd6d, recomputed today and matching; the asks-persist gate already covers it.
- NO-REWORK step 1: the only file in the repository naming Financial-2525 or FIN-## is the ask itself — nothing is shipped.

**Ambiguities → declared default.**
- **Month length: the sheets say M = 33 d (M∞(33), 1×/mth = 33 days, $99/M33, 4,762 $/M33); the messages say 30.333 ("30.333", "MoT=30.33", 91 ÷ 3). Which is the pay MoT and which is the budget's $/M basis?** → 30.333 = 91 ÷ 3 for the paycheck MoT AND for every $/M budget figure; the 33-day figures are carried as sheet data with their 2025.06.16 stamp, never used as a divisor. — _The later operator word wins; the ask binds quarter = 91 and 30.333 = 91 ÷ 3; CALENDARS.soi91.monthDays is already 91/3 in lib/soi-calendar.ts so no new constant is needed; a 33-day month gives 4 × 3 × 33 = 396 days, which cannot sit on the 364 + 1 year._
- **The real-time frames are written 33D / 66D / 99D with (30.3, 60.6, 90.9) beside them. Are the chart windows 33/66/99 days or thirds of the 91-day quarter?** → Windows = ⅓Q · ⅔Q · Q = 30.333 / 60.667 / 91 days, labelled with those fractions; the 33/66/99 names appear only as the sheet's aliases. — _The sheet's own parenthetical maps 33→30.3, 66→60.6, 99→90.9, and Q = 99 contradicts quarter = 91 in the same notes; thirds of 91 need no new number._
- **Year length: sheet says Y = 365 D and "Reference ↳ 365.25"; the ask says 4 × 91 with day 365 DOWN and a reset at perihelion. Is day 365 a fixed 24 h or does the year end at the next perihelion instant?** → Day 1 = PERIHELION_UTC[year]; days 1–364 are the four 91-day quarters; day 365 is the DOWN day and is ELASTIC — it ends at the next perihelion instant (2026: 9.28 h; 2027: 3.40 d) and the year's measured length is appended to the ledger. 365.25 is the Earth LTU reference for converting a MoT to another planet, not the calendar. — _"day resets everywhere at perihelion" is the ruling; the repo's PERIHELION_UTC table shows the instant jitters by more than a day year to year (Earth–Moon barycentre), so a fixed-length day 365 and a perihelion reset cannot both hold — perihelion wins._
- **Does the SoI day flip at the perihelion INSTANT (11:16 CST in 2026, so every day boundary sits at 11:16 CST) or at 00:00 CST of the perihelion date?** → The instant, exactly as soiYearStartUTC returns it; the 'CST standard' phrase governs display of every timestamp. — _UCRS HU 0 is an instant, not a date, and the operator invoked the celestial convention; if he prefers midnight CST that is one field in the domain JSON and a re-run of the calendar gate — worth asking because a person budgets by midnight, not 11:16._
- **"07:00" on 2026-10-01: CST fixed (UTC−6 → 13:00Z) or the bank app's wall clock (CDT, UTC−5 → 12:00Z)?** → 07:00 CST = 13:00Z, and the record prints CST and UTC side by side. — _His ruling is CST standard, no daylight shift; the difference is one hour = $4.95 of release at the paycheck rate, so it is asked once and then tamper-evident on the ledger._
- **Which date is `Start`: the operator's stated deposit (2026-10-01 07:00) or the bank's Posted 09/29/2026 (Status Pending)?** → Start = 2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST; `posted` = 2026-09-29 and `status` = pending → settled are provenance fields on the same record. — _"assign deposit date" is the operator's act; the bank row is the evidence and its Pending state is a real state the chart must show rather than hide._
- **"3 hrs later individual can withdraw": withdraw only what escrow has RELEASED by then (≈ $14.85), or draw against the whole deposit?** → Released only. The 3 h is a clearing hold on the deposit; after it, withdrawable = released − withdrawn; a withdrawal is its own appended transaction. — _"escrow that releases $/min" makes release the accrual; advancing unreleased money turns escrow into a loan, and the up-front-pay sentence belongs to the business frame that is test-later._
- **Does $/min release keep running on the DOWN day and across a quarter boundary (the paycheck crosses into Q4 on SoI day 274)?** → Yes. A MoT is a length; accrual is elapsed minutes (spreadPerMin); the calendar labels the chart and never pauses money. — _Pausing would silently shorten the payer's declared 30.333-day period — the class of defect the accrual invariant exists to refuse._
- **What is A in the A.B..C of a MoT: the whole period (position 0 → 3600.3600..3600), days (30.1200..0000), or hours (728.0000..0000)?** → The whole period. A = whole MoTs elapsed, B = 1/3600 of the MoT, C = 1/3600 of B; the length itself in days or hours is an LTU readout, printed under its own name. — _Only this reading makes "30.33 converted to 3600.3600..3600" true and planet-neutral (one orbit is 3600.3600..3600 whatever the period); format(91/3) and format(728) are what the shipped code gives today and neither yields the full unit._
- **The sheet declares 3600.0000..0000 · 3600.3600..3600 · 3599.3599..3599 "Equal"; the shipped parseABC gives 3601.0003 · 3600 · 3599.99999. Which wins?** → The sheet, by gate not by parse: 3600.3600..3600 is the NAME of the full unit (FULL_UNIT_ABC / FULL_ORBIT), never arithmetic; the end of a MoT is internally 1.0000..0000; 3599.3599..3599 is the last tick (one C ≈ 0.2 s short on a 30.333-day MoT) and prints as the end. — _abc-3600.ts already declares FULL_UNIT_ABC as a symbol with 'one whole A' semantics; a naïve parse of it overshoots the period by one B + one C, so equality must be asserted in code the way the operator wrote it._
- **The sheets write times as YYYY.MM.DD_HH.MM..SS and durations as 0000.00.33_00.00.00 — base-24/60 clock fields — while the law says everything is A.B..C Base-3600. Which grammar is the record's?** → Both, with named roles: the stamp grammar is the human LTU face of Start (and of a duration in D/h/m/s); the A.B..C is the position within the MoT; a gate proves each Start round-trips to one instant. — _The sheets use the stamp grammar on every line and the operator asks for A.B..C 'for time'; they only conflict if one is silently printed under the other's name._
- **Budget line names differ: sheet (Car, Electric, Food) vs addendum 3 (Auto, Utilities, Groceries, plus Dining Out); the sheet's Income 3,200 differs from the real $3,604.49 and its lines sum to a −200/M deficit.** → Addendum 3 names; sheet figures pre-filled as the operator's own example, each tagged with its source and stamp; Dining Out empty until he enters it; Income on the pilot = the real paycheck record, not 3,200. — _Later word wins for names; figures are data and are never invented or corrected — shown with their source so the −200 and the 3,200 read as what they are._
- **"tokenization 9999 HI TOKEN CAP": what in a financial record mints 웃?** → Nothing in revision 0.001; when tokenisation lands, 웃 = M × hours from the POD's witnessed clock and the cap is read from YUG_CEILING in lib/pod-yug.ts; elapsed-time $/min is never presented as a wage. — _$3,604.49 over 728 elapsed hours is $4.95/h, below the Texas floor — a false wage reading; pod-clock's rule that the clock span and the claim are never confused applies to money exactly._
- **Who holds the escrow in the personal pilot — the operator's employer pays his bank, not our escrow.** → A MODELLED escrow: the record computes escrowed/released/withdrawable from ($, Start, MoT) and no money moves; 'withdraw' is a recorded intent; no screen word implies the app holds funds. — _No payment rail is in scope (Stripe / Cube 14 sit with the business frame), and the panel may never claim more than happened._
- **Business months: sheet says 4-4-5 (28/28/35 = 91); the soi91 note says the same; the personal paycheck is an even third (30.333). Two month shapes on one quarter?** → Personal pilot = even thirds (the paycheck's own MoT); business frame = 4-4-5 weeks, declared as draft CRS rows and not implemented until the personal pilot has received feedback. — _The sheet files 4-4-5 under 'Test Later · Business'; addendum 5 fixes the order; both shapes sit on the same 91-day quarter so nothing is lost by waiting._

**MoT law, as this lens read it.** A transaction is ($, Start, MoT). MoT is a LENGTH, and in A.B..C that length is ONE WHOLE UNIT — the same way one orbit is 3600.3600..3600 whatever the planet's period (ucrs-2525 FULL_ORBIT; abc-3600 FULL_UNIT_ABC, 'one whole A'). For the pilot's paycheck: $ = 3,604.49 · Start = 2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST (13:00Z) · MoT = 30.333 d = 91 ÷ 3 = 728 h = 43,680 min = 2,620,800 s. Then A = whole MoTs elapsed (0 while the period runs, 1 at its end); B = 1/3600 of the MoT = 728 s = 12 min 8 s; C = 1/3600 of B = 0.2022 s. Position p = elapsed ÷ MoT, written by toABC(p): 3 h after deposit = 0.0014..3007; one day = 0.0118..2453; the end = 1.0000..0000, which the sheet names 3600.3600..3600 — and also 3600.0000..0000 and 3599.3599..3599 (the last tick, one C ≈ 0.2 s before the end): three spellings, one instant, asserted by a gate because the shipped parser reads them as 3601.0003 / 3600 / 3599.99999. Released $ = $ × abcToValue(position): $1.00125 per B, $0.000278 per C, $14.85 at +3 h, $118.83 at one day, exactly $3,604.49 at 3600.3600..3600. Earth LTU faces of the same law: $/D 118.83 · $/h 4.9512 · $/min 0.082520 (= spreadPerMin(3604.49, 91/3)) · $/s 0.0013753; ladder D = 1 · W = 7 · M = 91/3 · Q = 91 · Y = 364 + DOWN day, minutes via calMinutes / CADENCE_UNIT (Q M W D exist; Y and h/min/s are the gap to add). The length's own LTU readouts — format(91/3) = 30.1200..0000 as days, format(728) = 728.0000..0000 as hours — are printed under their own names and are never the position. The sheets' 2026.10.01_07.00..00 and 0000.00.33_00.00.00 are the clock-face grammar (YYYY.MM.DD_HH.MM..SS), a third thing, kept as the human face of Start. Calendar face on the fixed CST clock: perihelion 2026 = 2026-01-03 11:16 CST = SoI Day 1; the deposit lands on SoI day 271 (Q3 day 89); Q4 opens at day 274 = 2026-10-03 11:16 CST; the MoT ends 2026-10-31 15:00 CST = SoI day 302 (Q4 day 29); the bank's Posted 09/29 = day 269; day 365 (DOWN) opens 2027-01-02 11:16 CST and ends at the next perihelion 20:33 CST the same day (9.28 h; in 2027 the same span is 3.40 d). Mars: the same 0 → 3600.3600..3600 over a MoT declared in sols; only the LTU face changes, as ucrsAt does with ltu = spotu × tSec.

**Chart.** axes: x = elapsed time inside the chosen window, ticked in fixed CST with the SoI day and quarter under each tick (Day 271 · Q3 d89) and the active MoT's A.B..C position along the bottom (0.0000..0000 → 3600.3600..3600); y = dollars on the left, with $/min as the live read-out beside the cursor. Baseline unit is $/D (sheet 1: 'Baseline: $/Day'), $/min and $/s are the unlocked finer faces. · series: escrowed — declining line from $3,604.49 at Start to $0 at 3600.3600..3600 (escrow remaining) · released — the mirror, rising at $0.082520/min; released(t) = $ × position(t) · withdrawable — zero until Start + 3 h and until the deposit is settled, then released − withdrawn · withdrawn / cash on hand — a step series, one step per appended withdrawal ('Outcome: Cash On Hand') · budget burn — the addendum-3 lines as $/min, Fixed solid and Variable dashed, drawn as a declining commitment against released; the sheet's 'Swings' rendered as Δ $/D steps (144 → 138 → 137) · net — released − budget burn, the one number the tile shows · marks: Start: vertical mark labelled `$3,604.49 · 2026.10.01_07.00..00 CST · 30.333` · +3 h: 'withdrawable from' mark at 10:00 CST with the released amount then ($14.85) · MoT end: 3600.3600..3600 mark at 2026-10-31 15:00 CST · Quarter boundary: SoI day 274 (2026-10-03 11:16 CST) thin mark 'Q4' · DOWN day: shaded band for day 365 with its measured length (9.28 h in the 2026 year) · now: cursor carrying the live A.B..C position, $ released, $/min · deposit status badge: PENDING until settled, then SETTLED — never 'complete' while pending · every colour from the 13-Trinity spectrum only (lib/trinity-palette.ts), dark ground by default · states: escrowed → released (continuous, per elapsed minute, never decreasing, never above $) → released → withdrawable (requires now ≥ Start + 3 h AND deposit settled) → withdrawable → withdrawn (an appended transaction with its own $, Start, MoT = 0; never edited) → deposit: pending → settled (provenance state from the bank record, shown, never hidden) · cadence: The released curve and the tile advance every second on the phone (the $/s face, $0.0014/s, is visible moving); the $/min read-out re-derives each minute; the record itself changes only when a transaction is appended. Everything drawn is a pure function of (now, ledger) — derived on read like pod-clock measure(), never a stored running total. · phone: At 375 px: one stat tile first (released now · $/min 0.0825 · position 0.0014..3007 · withdrawable), the chart full-width beneath with a ⅓Q / ⅔Q / Q window toggle, marks as thin verticals with a legend row instead of inline labels, 16 px gutters, no horizontal scroll, touch targets ≥ 44 px, all strings via t() in 33 languages, no database/migration/vendor words on the screen, the R-CORE badge mounted last in normal flow.

**Invariants.**
- "Each transaction gets amount, Deposit day and time, and MoT length of time" — a record missing $, Start or MoT is refused before it persists; a MoT of zero is only a withdrawal. — `fin-record-shape (tests/fin-record.test.mjs, asserted before the write like assertSource)`
- "escrow that releases $/min" — released never decreases while the MoT runs, never exceeds $, and equals $ exactly at 3600.3600..3600; the fixture $3,604.49 · 30.333 reads $0.082520/min and $14.85 at +3 h. — `fin-accrual-law (tests/fin-accrual.test.mjs)`
- "3600.0000..0000 · 3600.3600..3600 · 3599.3599..3599 } Equal" — all three spellings resolve to the end of the MoT and the end prints as FULL_UNIT_ABC. — `fin-abc-full-unit (tests/fin-abc.test.mjs)`
- "A.B..C is used for time and easily converted to Earth local time units LTU" — every position round-trips A.B..C ↔ s/min/h/D/W/M/Q/Y within one C, on a fixed CST clock that never shifts for daylight. — `fin-ltu-roundtrip (tests/fin-ltu.test.mjs)`
- "day resets everywhere at perihelion each year" — Day 1 = PERIHELION_UTC[year], quarters are 91 days, day 365 is DOWN and elastic, and 2026-10-01 07:00 CST reads SoI day 271 · Q3 d89. — `fin-calendar-perihelion (tests/fin-calendar.test.mjs)`
- "3 hrs later individual can withdraw" — withdrawable is zero before Start + 3 h and never more than released − withdrawn; a withdrawal is appended, never edited. — `fin-escrow-states (tests/fin-escrow.test.mjs)`
- "revision control using the same framework as drone 2525" — revisions strictly increase, a release cites a commit, project.revision is the last entry, generated views match the source, the ledger's rev is monotonic, the handoff sha equals the ask's sidecar. — `fin-crs + financial-render --check + fin-ledger (tests/fin-crs.test.mjs, scripts/financial-render.mjs --check, tests/fin-ledger.test.mjs)`
- "9999 HI TOKEN CAP" — the only ceiling in Financial-2525 is YUG_CEILING imported from lib/pod-yug.ts; a second literal 9999 fails the build. — `fin-one-ceiling (source-level, the pod-invariant pattern)`
- "each user gets their own log in" — a record belongs to one login and is never merged into or read from another's ledger; a device never evicts a financial entry to make room. — `fin-tenant-scope (tests/fin-store.test.mjs)`
- Money is the operator's real paycheck and figures are data — every dollar literal in the Financial-2525 source traces to the domain JSON or the ledger; the sheet's arithmetic slips are rendered beside the checked figure, never replaced. — `fin-figures-are-data (source-level grep of $ literals against the domain JSON)`
- The person never meets the machine — no database, migration, vendor or model word reaches the personal screen; the panel never says 'complete' while the deposit is pending. — `fin-lexicon-voice (the sign-lexicon-voice pattern) + rcore-revisions (redactModelIds already in the adapter)`

**Reuse.**

- frontend/lib/ucrs-2525.ts — FULL_ORBIT, ucrsAt (ltu = spotu × tSec, the Mars path), tzFromLon (CST −6), fmtClock, PLANETS tDays

- frontend/lib/pod-clock.ts — the derive-on-read measure() pattern for an append-only event log, hhmmss, the 'two numbers never confused' rule
- frontend/lib/pod-yug.ts — YUG_CEILING (the one 9,999), mint, settle, standing, stamp/Vintage for when tokenisation lands
- frontend/lib/pod-store.ts — appendPod / replayPod / podHistory (per-device append-only ledger; the eviction rule must NOT be inherited for money)

- scripts/drone-render.mjs and scripts/drs-render.mjs — assertSource / --check / no-undefined render guard: extract the shared class into one module used by drone, drs and financial instead of a third copy
- docs/traceability/drone-2525.ledger.json — the append-only ledger shape for docs/traceability/financial-2525.ledger.json
