**Risks.**
- CONFIRMED by execution: a withdrawal dated after the MoT for the whole amount validates (checked against the future balance); a $10 withdrawal at +3 h then also validates; at +32 d the record holds 361,449 cents withdrawn against 360,449 deposited and availableCents = Math.max(0, …) reports 0 — the 'never negative' invariant is clamped, not held.
- CONFIRMED by execution: the hold, the release and every balance read the phone's Date.now(); with the device clock advanced 3 h, 1,485 cents are withdrawable at the deposit instant. A diary today; the escrow fraud the moment any rail, employer message or business pre-funding pays on that number.
- CONFIRMED by execution: a deposit backdated 40 days is 100 % withdrawable at entry — the payee stamps the payer's deposit; acceptable only as a self-record and only if badged as such; disqualifying for the business frame.
- A tampered device record reads as empty and the next deposit's saveRecord overwrites the same key — the person's damaged history is destroyed by recording again, while fin.chain_broken promises it is 'never repaired silently' (the AAR 2026-09-09 class: a record discarded on a failure path).
- The chain hash is FNV-1a, two 32-bit lanes, computed by a library that ships in the public bundle: evidence against accident, none against an adversary or the person themself; 'Chain verified' must never be read by a payer as proof.
- Privacy: the operator's real paycheck, the masked payroll descriptor and his budget lines are in a PUBLIC repository (unauthenticated API returns 200) and are served to every visitor of the live site before sign-in via domain.gen.ts; it is his worked example, but consent is inferred from the ask, not written on the record, and nothing stops a later edition replacing the example with a user's row.
- Storage: the record is cleartext JSON under exel-fin:<auth0 sub> — the key itself reveals the identity provider and subject; any script on the origin, a shared device or browser sync reads amounts, memos and payer; Auth0's token cache is also cacheLocation=localstorage; no encryption at rest.
- Isolation: no financial table exists in Supabase (good — no leak path yet), but FIN-08's stretch names financial_transactions and the app's only isolation is a bearer owner_key with auth.uid() null for every request; a money table on that model is readable by whoever holds a key that lives in localStorage.
- The memo is free text with no refusal: the person will paste the bank descriptor or an unmasked account number and it renders in the roster and ledger.
- Silent dedupe: id = d-<when>-<cents> collapses two identical real deposits into one with no message; the form clears as if recorded.
- The person-facing string fin.device_only names Supabase and 'the login being wired' — the person meets the machine on their money screen (the sign-voice law, which today gates only signer strings).
- Two answers for one MoT: an instant's A.B..C uses the sourced 364.39-d interval and a length uses the 365.26-d mean, so the chart's reveal axis spans 299.7 A while the label says 298.97 A; and two perihelion tables (soi-calendar seed vs calendar.ts) disagree for 2028 by 17 minutes.
- Efficiency: every 1 Hz tick recomputes balanceAt for 120 samples × all transactions; fine for one paycheck, a phone-draining quadratic when the same surface carries a business ledger.
- Business frame (declared 'test later'): the hold is 3 h from the DEPOSIT, not from each release, so after hour three everything released is withdrawable at once — the business's exposure is the whole released amount at any time (FD-03); a payer message that is not a settlement plus the payee's phone clock plus any rail is the classic pre-funding fraud; none of this may be built before the clock and stamper questions are answered.
- Process: at review start r.003 was mid-edit with financial-render --check refusing ('0.003 is not the last entry'); it is now committed and pushed at 13028c0 with gates green — but LIVE is UNVERIFIED from the sandbox, and the operator must be told SHA · pushed · UNVERIFIED, never 'shipped'.

**The three things revision 0.001 must do first.**
- State and gate the money invariants at EVERY instant, fixing the class not the instance: add frontend/tests/financial-invariant.test.mjs to test:ci (the pod-invariant pattern) that (a) refuses a withdrawal dated after now or before the latest entry and re-validates at the frontier so availableCents is never clamped up from a negative, (b) proves a broken-chain store is moved aside under exel-fin:<owner>:broken:<stamp> before any write, (c) asserts no payout path reads Date.now() and every typed row renders SELF-RECORDED with the phone-time caveat, and (d) gives ids a rev nonce and says 'already recorded' on a no-op append — the four typed-instant / clamped-balance / overwritten-store / silent-dedupe members of one class, fixed together in lib/financial-2525/accrual.ts, record.ts and components/financial-2525/command-ux1.tsx.
- Decide and record the privacy and isolation posture before any cloud half: write example.consent ('operator 2026-09-30, addendum 2') into docs/financial-2525/financial-2525.v.000.json with a financial-crs assertion that EXAMPLE.payer stays 'example'; add a financial-voice gate (sign-lexicon-voice pattern, all 33 languages) that refuses account-shaped digit runs in memos and retires 'Supabase' and 'login being wired' from fin.device_only in favour of the person's terms; and write FIN-08's stretch as a migration that ships ONLY with Auth0-in-Supabase (auth.uid() policy alone, no bearer policy on money) — never a financial_transactions table under owner_key.
- Keep the edition honest and reuse what exists: report r.003 as SHA 13028c0 · committed · pushed · LIVE UNVERIFIED with the site URL; make the MoT length use the same revolution interval as the instant (or label both) and fold calendar.ts's sourced perihelion rows into lib/soi-calendar.ts so there is one table; replace record.ts's device half with pod-store's append/evict pattern and the component's usd() with architect-economy's fmtUsd; memoize the chart series per sample bucket so the 1 Hz tick moves only the number and the NOW line — and persist this twelve-lens round beside r.003 under docs/financial-2525/reviews/ (the record FIN-09 names does not exist yet).
